# RPX Tracking: Complete Per-Scene and Per-Phase Results

Frame budget: **250**. Each row is one evaluated scene-phase cell. HOTA, DetA, AssA, IDF1, and MOTA are percentages. Higher is better for those metrics; lower is better for IDSW and latency.

## Coverage

| Protocol | Models | Cells/model | Scenes/model | Total complete cells | Phases |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|
| MOS | 8 | 300 | 100 | 2,400 | 0, 1, 2 |
| Ego | 8 | 100 | 100 | 800 | 0 |

The complete tables below contain **3,200 benchmark cells**. The incomplete MOTIP run is excluded from the complete tables and retained in a separate appendix.
